## Jaffe–Witten section 6.5: an actual finite periodic SU(2) Wilson probability

- **Public source and version:** Arthur Jaffe and Edward Witten, *Quantum Yang–Mills Theory*, published in *The Millennium Prize Problems*, American Mathematical Society, 2006; the publication year is confirmed by Arthur Jaffe's author-maintained bibliography https://www.arthurjaffe.com/Assets/documents/PapersToDownload.htm . The exact consulted artifact is the Clay-hosted fourteen-page PDF https://www.claymath.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/yangmills.pdf , section 6.5, PDF pages 11–12, consulted 2026-10-28. That artifact has no explicit revision stamp in the inspected text; no byte identity with another edition is asserted. The cited method is the compact-group, compactified finite lattice integral and its distinction from lattice-spacing/infinite-volume limits. No historical influence or researcher participation is inferred.
- **Local construction and normalization:** grwtsk contributors. Use the actual `Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup (Fin 2) ℂ`, not a finite surrogate gauge group. Vertices are `Fin 4 → ZMod L`, positive links are `Vertex L × Fin 4`, and plaquettes are a vertex and a pair `mu<nu`. The oriented square uses `U(x,mu) U(x+e_mu,nu) U(x+e_nu,mu)^(-1) U(x,nu)^(-1)`. Vertex gauges act by `h(x) U(x,mu) h(x+e_mu)^(-1)`. The penalty convention is explicitly `phi(g)=1-Re Tr(g)/2`; this local SU(2) normalization is not claimed to be a formula stated verbatim in the survey.
- **Exact hypotheses and boundaries:** the lattice group algebra is valid for every natural L. Finite inventories and their measure construction require `NeZero L`; the terminal existence consumer explicitly assumes `3<=L` and derives that instance. Partition-function bounds and probability require real beta>=0. Fixed-gauge measure invariance is proved for every real beta. Configurations and vertex gauges are the full actual finite products; no gauge fixing or quotient measure is substituted. All links in the oriented plaquette, including both inverse links, are retained.
- **Proof and actual consumer:** torus translations commute, so group cancellation gives plaquette conjugation covariance at the initial vertex. Matrix trace cyclicity gives action invariance. Actual unitarity bounds each matrix entry by one, hence real trace in[-2,2] and phi in[0,2]. The closed determinant-one unitary matrix subset is contained in the compact product of closed complex unit disks, proving actual compactness and topological-group instances. Normalize maintained Haar on the actual compact univ; mass is one. Right invariance is derived by compact Haar uniqueness and total mass one, with no commutativity claim. `Measure.pi` constructs the link product Haar probability. Its coordinatewise two-sided invariance proves actual gauge preservation. The finite action satisfies0<=S<=2*card(P). Bounded measurable integration yields exp(-2*beta*card(P))<=Z<=1 and Z>0. The actual measure is `nu.withDensity (ofReal (exp(-beta*S)/Z))`; its total mass one and gauge invariance are proved. No invariant measure, positive partition function or probability property is supplied as an unproved research field.
- **Exact source binding:** complete inspected base main `b870bf85c47e9e9bfedf3fc1fd31a2be60719e76`, tree `59ae81e242a9e1d51fd2462a92ec34cfe66e2c9b`; the six candidate source blobs below specify the overlay exactly. These are uploaded source candidates pending human integration, not a new main commit. Earlier bytes and external acceptance do not certify these candidates.

## Jaffe–Witten finite Wilson regulator: bond reflection and the Hermitian observable pairing

- **Public paper context:** Arthur Jaffe and Edward Witten, *Quantum Yang–Mills Theory*, published in *The Millennium Prize Problems*, American Mathematical Society, 2006; same Clay-hosted fourteen-page version, section 6.5, pages 11–12, https://www.claymath.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/yangmills.pdf . The earlier ledger entry records the author-maintained bibliography and version qualification. Section 6.5 separates finite lattice measures, reflection positivity and reconstruction, limits and a physical gap. This increment derives the user-specified finite bond-reflection identities locally; it does not import the survey's reflection-positivity statement without proof or infer historical influence or researcher participation.
- **Exact finite model:** use the already published actual matrix SU(2) Wilson density at period L=2*T with T>=2, real beta>=0, and time coordinate 0. Vertex reflection has time 1-x_0 modulo 2*T and fixed spatial coordinates. A forward temporal link is reflected to the inverse positive link based at theta(x)-e_0; a spatial link is pulled back to theta(x) without inversion. The complete link and plaquette inventories and the actual normalized product Haar measure are retained.
- **Reflection and action proof:** actual vertex and link involutions give the configuration involution and homeomorphism. At a temporal plaquette, put y=theta(x)-e_0 and A=U(y,0). The literal transformed holonomy is A^(-1)*H_U(y;0,i)^(-1)*A. Spatial plaquettes pull back at theta(x). Matrix inversion is star, so actual real-trace identities prove phi(g^(-1))=phi(g). Conjugation invariance and an explicit bijection of the complete plaquette inventory prove S(Theta U)=S(U), including orientation reversal and both periodic boundaries.
- **Actual measure proof:** inversion of the noncommutative SU(2) group is not used as a homomorphism. The inverse pushforward of the constructed Haar probability is left invariant by actual right invariance; compact Haar uniqueness and mass one prove inversion preservation. Selective temporal-coordinate inversion and finite coordinate permutation each preserve the actual product Haar measure. Their composition is literally Theta. The action equality then proves reflection invariance of the constructed Wilson density. Gauge invariance is not substituted for this proof. These symmetry identities work at every nonzero period and every real beta; probability/integrability usage retains beta>=0.
- **Positive-time algebra:** positive vertices have actual ZMod representatives 1,...,T. A positive interior link has both endpoints in that set. The concrete complex subalgebra of continuous functions consists of observables invariant under all actual vertex gauges and equal on configurations agreeing on those links. Constants, addition, multiplication and conjugation closure are proved. Temporal crossing layers based at 0 and T are explicitly excluded from observable dependence. At time T the target has representative T+1<2*T, proved using T>=2. The crossing variables remain in every full Haar/Wilson integration; no measure is projected or truncated. Reflection exchanges the positive and negative regions, so positive-algebra reflection stability is not assumed.
- **Actual consumer and hypotheses:** for T>=2, beta>=0 and members F,G of this concrete algebra, define B(F,G)=integral conjugate(F(Theta U))*G(U) dmu_beta,2T. Continuity on actual compact configuration space and the proved Wilson probability give integrability. Change of variables under the actual measure-preserving involution, followed by integral conjugation, gives B(F,G)=conjugate(B(G,F)) and Im B(F,F)=0; an equivalent equality identifies the diagonal with its real part. The positiveWilsonMeasure definition is exactly the existing period 2*T Wilson measure with NeZero derived from T>=2. No integrability or measure-invariance field is assumed. The source proves these pairing identities, not a packaged sesquilinear-form structure or a Hilbert norm.

## Jaffe–Witten finite Wilson regulator: local positive-type kernel and the two-boundary gluing frontier

- **Public paper context:** Arthur Jaffe and Edward Witten, *Quantum Yang–Mills Theory*, in *The Millennium Prize Problems*, American Mathematical Society, 2006; the same Clay-hosted fourteen-page source, section 6.5, pages 11–12, https://www.claymath.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/yangmills.pdf . The finite lattice/reflection-positivity context is retained. The elementary Frobenius/entrywise-series proof below is derived locally for the user's exact normalization; no theorem or historical influence is attributed to the survey without an explicit source argument, and no researcher participation is inferred.
- **Exact kernel and hypotheses:** the existing SU(2) is the determinant-one unitary matrix group on Fin 2 over Complex, with penalty(g)=1-Re Tr(g)/2. For every finite index type I (including empty), beta>=0, A:I->SU2 and arbitrary complex c:I->Complex, w_beta(A,B)=exp(-beta*penalty(A*B^(-1))). The actual ordered sum Q=sum_i sum_j conjugate(c_i)*c_j*w_beta(A_i,A_j) satisfies Im Q=0 and Re Q>=0. No injectivity, distinctness, coefficient reality or strict positivity is assumed.
- **Proof and normalization:** inversion is actual matrix star. All four complex entries, both real coordinates per entry, give Re Tr(A*B^*)=sum_k f(A)_k*f(B)_k in eight real coordinates. Thus w=exp(-beta)*exp((beta/2)*Gram). Multiplying an entrywise Gram power by one more Gram factor expands into a sum of the previous quadratic form with coordinate-weighted coefficients. Power zero is the all-ones kernel, not the identity matrix. Nonnegative Taylor coefficients and the scalar exponential-series limit at every entry, including negative Gram entries, give positivity of the entrywise exponential. For complex coefficients its real quadratic form is the sum of the real-coefficient forms of Re c and Im c; symmetry cancels the imaginary form. This is a scalar entrywise exponential, never a matrix exponential. Zero Taylor truncation is the zero kernel; the zeroth power is one. beta=0 is all ones. The explicit repeated-matrix family (A,A), coefficients (1,-1), has quadratic sum zero for every beta.

- **Public paper context:** Arthur Jaffe and Edward Witten, *Quantum Yang–Mills Theory*, in *The Millennium Prize Problems*, American Mathematical Society, 2006; same Clay-hosted fourteen-page source, section 6.5, pages 11–12, https://www.claymath.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/yangmills.pdf . - **Literal link partition:** E+ is the existing two-endpoint positive interior predicate. E- is its actual reflected pullback. C is exactly the temporal links based at representatives 0 or T. Temporal E+ bases are 1,...,T-1, temporal E- bases T+1,...,2*T-1; spatial E+ bases are 1,...,T and spatial E- bases 0,T+1,...,2*T-1. Proved ZMod successor, negative and 1-minus representative formulas establish coverage and all pairwise exclusions, including wraparound and T=2. The actual finite bijection (E+ disjoint_union E+) disjoint_union C -> E sends the second positive copy through the maintained reflected-link involution.
- **Actual Haar disintegration:** J(u,v,t) retains u on E+, the reflected v on E- (inverse on temporal coordinates), and every actual t coordinate on C. Each finite sum split, index bijection and temporal inverse is proved measure preserving from the constructed one-link Haar probability. The full coordinate map is a measurable equivalence; its product-Haar change of variables and finite-probability/compact-continuity Fubini proofs give actual disintegration with the complete crossing integration. No coordinate is silently deleted.
- **Concrete conditional gauge:** h is inverse t on negative time0, t from the preceding time-T edge on negative timeT+1, identity on positive vertices and elsewhere. The previous-time map and all shifted endpoints are proved on the actual torus. At base0 the cancellation is t^(-1)*t; at baseT it is t*t^(-1). T>=2 makes the modular negative layers distinct. The induced second-half transformation has multipliers h(theta(source)) and h(theta(target))^(-1), acting on each coordinate by fixed left/right Haar translations for fixed t. An explicit measurable equivalence and actual product-Haar invariance supply its conditional change of variables.
- **Conditional integration theorem:** `GRWTSK.Geometry.GaugeTheory.WilsonBoundaryGaugeIntegration.two_boundary_gauge_haar_gluing` proves integral I(U) dnu(U) = integral integral I(J(u,v,1)) dnu+(u)dnu+(v) for continuous gauge-invariant I. The full action is the wholly-positive action plus its reflected action plus the sum over all actual temporal crossing plaquettes at both boundaries. The negative sum uses the existing reflected-plaquette bijection and literal penalty reflection theorem, including inverse orientation. Thus S(J(u,v,1))=S+(u)+S+(v)+S_cross(J(u,v,1)); no factorization field supplies this equality.

## Jaffe–Witten finite Wilson regulator: boundary-kernel integration and finite reflection positivity

- **Public paper context:** Arthur Jaffe and Edward Witten, *Quantum Yang–Mills Theory*, in *The Millennium Prize Problems*, American Mathematical Society, 2006; same Clay-hosted fourteen-page source, section 6.5, pages 11–12, https://www.claymath.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/yangmills.pdf . This stage derives the local finite-model reflection-positivity statement through actual feature moments and Haar integrals. No survey theorem, historical influence, researcher participation, continuum construction or mass-gap conclusion is inferred from the context.
- **Exact finite-model consumer:** natural T>=2, period2*T, real beta>=0, existing actual matrix SU(2), existing penalty 1-Re Tr(g)/2, the actual full Wilson measure/partition/reflection and F in the existing positiveTimeAlgebra T. The source theorem `GRWTSK.Geometry.GaugeTheory.WilsonReflectionPositivity.finiteWilson_reflection_positive` concludes Re(positiveReflectionForm T hT beta F F)>=0. NeZero(2*T) is constructed from T>=2 in its proof. No positivity, Schur-product, gluing, normalization, bounded-feature-margin or physical-gap certificate is assumed.
- **Exact concatenation and multiplicity:** BoundaryCoordinate T = CrossPlaquette T x RealCoordinate, retaining all eight real matrix-entry coordinates for every actual crossing plaquette at both boundaries. Phi(u)(b,k)=realCoordinates(u(boundaryPositiveLink b),k). Neither repeated positive spatial indices nor repeated feature words are deduplicated. With P=card(CrossPlaquette T), direct expansion of the existing kernel product gives K_boundary(u,v)=exp(-beta*P)*exp((beta/2)*Gram(Phi,u,v)). These are scalar entrywise exponentials. The actual SU(2) real-trace range supplies |Gram|<=2*P. The same identity consumes the existing generic finite exponential-Gram theorem to prove finite positive type for arbitrary finite complex coefficients, without a Schur-product bridge.
- **Integrated tensor words:** `FiniteRealGramIntegral.gram_pow_words` expands each n-th Gram power as the finite sum over all words w:Fin n->Coordinate of wordFeature(w,u)*wordFeature(w,v), where wordFeature is the actual product of coordinate values. Scalar complex-coefficient integration is exact: integral_(u,v) conjugate(g(v))*g(u)*Gram(Phi,u,v)^n dnu+(u)dnu+(v) = sum_w |integral_u g(u)*wordFeature(w,u) dnu+(u)|^2. Finite sum/integral interchange uses proved continuity and integrability on the actual compact probability space; actual product integration and integral conjugation produce each squared complex norm. The empty word contributes |integral g|^2, and the zeroth-power kernel is all ones, not the identity.
- **Taylor and limit premises paid:** beta/2>=0 makes every Taylor coefficient nonnegative, so the real part of every integrated truncation is nonnegative. The scalar-series convergence already written in the exact source is transported to Complex pointwise. The generic bound |Taylor_n(t*Gram)|<=exp(t*R), together with the continuous-map sup norm, gives the explicit integrable product-space constant ||g||_infinity^2*exp(t*R). In the actual model t=beta/2 and R=2*P, hence the valid fixed-T majorant is ||g||_infinity^2*exp(beta*P). Every truncation is AEStronglyMeasurable by actual continuity/integrability. The pinned Bochner dominated-convergence theorem and closed real order pass the integral inequality to the exponential. The source additionally proves |exp(-beta*P)*Taylor_n((beta/2)*Gram)|<=1. No positivity is inferred from pointwise K>=0, and no continuum-uniform coercivity or lower bound is supplied by these upper bounds.
- **Actual boundary integral theorem:** `GRWTSK.Geometry.GaugeTheory.WilsonBoundaryKernelIntegral.boundaryKernelProduct_integral_nonnegative` quantifies every continuous complex g on the actual HalfConfig T. It proves Re(integral_u integral_v conjugate(g(v))*g(u)*K_boundary(u,v) dnu+(v)dnu+(u))>=0. Its measure is exactly the existing normalized product Haar, with compact/Borel/second-countable structure inherited from the finite SU(2) coordinates. Full product and outer integrability are supplied separately; the double integral follows by actual Fubini. No new invariant-measure field is introduced.
- **Concrete weighted observable and return:** continuity of the finite positive plaquette action is proved from the actual four-link holonomies, fixed positive-plaquette inventory and SU(2) penalty. The existing J(u,1,1) map supplies continuity of halfAction and halfObservable. Thus g(u)=exp(-beta*halfAction(u))*halfObservable(F,u) is constructed as an actual continuous complex map, with single and product integrability proved. Its kernel integrand equals the published exact two-boundary gluing integrand, by real-weight conjugation and scalar exp_add. The existing gluing identity returns the nonnegative integral to Z*B(F,F). Actual Z>0 from the existing normalization theorem yields Re B(F,F)>=0. The companion properties theorem includes actual normalized integrability, zero imaginary diagonal and nonnegative real part.
- **Scope:** this is semidefiniteness for the specified finite periodic model, including beta=0, zero observables and possible null observables. No strictly positive norm, positive spectral margin, Hilbert quotient/completion, time evolution, vacuum/entire ground sector, infinite-volume/continuum scaling or physical mass gap is constructed. ## Finite SU(2) Wilson reflection form: sesquilinearity, radical and algebraic quotient

- **Paper context/bylines:** Arthur Jaffe and Edward Witten, *Quantum Yang--Mills Theory*, in *The Millennium Prize Problems*, AMS (2006), section 6.5, pp. 11--12; stable source https://www.claymath.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/yangmills.pdf. The paper distinguishes lattice regulators, reconstruction, infinite-volume/continuum limits and a physical mass gap. This increment prepares the finite reconstruction algebra from the exact maintained Wilson integral. The particular algebraic proofs below are local deductions, not an assertion that the paper formalizes them or that either author participated. Local attribution: grwtsk contributors. No historical influence is inferred.
- **Exact model and hypotheses:** fixed natural T>=2, period L=2*T, beta>=0, the existing actual SU(2) matrix group, Wilson penalty 1-Re Tr/2, normalized constructed Wilson measure, literal bond reflection, and the existing complex positiveTimeAlgebra T of continuous gauge-invariant observables depending on positive interior links. Every link, both crossing layers 0,T and the inherited Haar integrations remain present. No new measure, positivity or integrability field is assumed.
- **Actual integral laws and convention:** the mixed-product integrability theorem supplies each summand required by integral_add in either slot. Pointwise conjugation and the scalar integral identity give B(zF,G)=conj(z)B(F,G) and B(F,zG)=zB(F,G). The actual B is bundled as positiveTimeAlgebra T ->[starRingEnd Complex] (positiveTimeAlgebra T ->[Complex] Complex), namely a conjugate-linear first map with linear second maps. The scalar identities themselves hold for every real beta; the bundled additive map uses beta>=0 for actual mixed integrability.
- **Imported generic mathematics and its exact use:** Mathlib's semilinear-form convention retains Andreas Swerdlow's authorship; its pre-inner-product core and semidefinite Cauchy--Schwarz proof retain Zhouhang Zhou, Sébastien Gouëzel and Frédéric Dupuis's authorship. The submodule quotient and liftQ retain Johannes Hölzl, Mario Carneiro, Kevin Buzzard and Yury Kudryashov's authorship. All retain Apache-2.0 upstream license. The pinned PreInnerProductSpace.Core is used only as algebraic data for InnerProductSpace.Core.inner_mul_inner_self_le. Neither its toNorm nor any derived seminormed-group, topology or inner-product-space instance is installed on the already normed observable algebra.
- **Cauchy--Schwarz consumer:** the existing actual Hermiticity and finiteWilson_reflection_positive supply the core fields. Its pure algebraic inequality, together with norm-conjugation, gives norm(B(F,G))^2 <= Re B(F,F)*Re B(G,G). The maintained generic proof covers zero diagonals; no division by a diagonal is used, and its off-diagonal division occurs only after proving that scalar norm nonzero.
- **The actual radical:** nullSubmodule is the canonical LinearMap.ker of reflectionSesqForm. Cauchy--Schwarz proves membership iff Re B(F,F)=0. If the diagonal vanishes, norm(B(F,G))^2<=0 and hence B(F,G)=0 for every G; Hermiticity supplies the other slot. Thus the requested zero-diagonal set is proved to be a complex submodule, and both-slot null-addition invariance is an actual consumer. No positivity certificate or closure assumption defines it.
- **Actual algebraic quotient consumer:** ReflectionQuotient is the maintained Submodule quotient of the same positive-time algebra by that exact kernel. One liftQ descends each linear second slot using right annihilation; a second semilinear liftQ descends the first slot using left annihilation. quotientReflectionForm(mk F,mk G)=B(F,G) definitionally. The difference conditions F-F' in N and G-G' in N give representative independence. Quotient induction returns the inherited Hermiticity, real diagonal and nonnegativity to actual representatives. Re quotientReflectionForm(q,q)=0 iff q=0; the diagonal is strictly positive when q is nonzero. This is a vector-space quotient only: the null submodule is not asserted to be an ideal, and no quotient algebra is introduced.

## Finite SU(2) Wilson: two-step-margin translation descends to the null quotient

- **Paper context/bylines:** Arthur Jaffe and Edward Witten, *Quantum Yang--Mills Theory*, in *The Millennium Prize Problems*, AMS (2006), section 6.5, pp. 11--12; stable source https://www.claymath.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/yangmills.pdf. The source separates finite lattice regulators, reconstruction, infinite-volume/continuum limits and a physical gap. This increment is a local finite-model algebraic construction using the maintained Wilson measure and null quotient. It neither attributes these particular proofs to the paper nor infers historical influence or researcher participation. Local attribution: grwtsk contributors.
- **Actual hypotheses and source:** T is natural with T>=3, period L=2*T, beta>=0 for the radical/quotient consumers. The configuration, actual SU(2) matrix group, penalty 1-Re Tr/2, product Haar and normalized Wilson measure, bond reflection, positiveTimeAlgebra and nullSubmodule are unchanged. Translation and the unrestricted integral identity themselves hold at every nonzero period and every real beta as specified by their declarations. The exact existing mixed-observable integrability and finite reflection-positivity chain remain the suppliers for the positive form and radical.
- **Literal translation and incidences:** S U(x,mu)=U(x+e0,mu), with the inverse subtracting one modulo L. Explicit bijections of vertices, full positive links and full mu<nu plaquettes preserve directions and all inventories. Holonomy translation retains all four link factors, including both inverses. The vertex gauge becomes h'(x)=h(x+e0); commutation of the actual periodic shifts proves the target-endpoint equality. Temporal reflection retains its inverse link; the spatial and temporal base calculations separately prove S Theta S=Theta. Neither crossing layer 0,T nor any Haar variable is removed.
- **Actual measure construction consumed:** the link bijection instantiates the maintained proved product-Haar reindexing theorem. The plaquette bijection reindexes the complete literal action sum, giving action invariance. The existing Boltzmann-density theorem then proves preservation of the actual normalized Wilson measure. Gauge invariance is not substituted for this coordinate change, and no invariant-measure or translation certificate is assumed. The actual translation homeomorphism supplies its measurable embedding for the Bochner change of variables.
- **Two steps of support room:** twoStepDomain T is a complex submodule of the existing positive algebra, defined by dependence only on links whose TWO endpoints have natural time representatives in 1,...,T-2. The exact no-wrap calculation gives val(x+e0)=val(x)+1 and val(x+2e0)=val(x)+2 for those vertices. These values and the translated targets are positive and at most T. Consequently translatePositive and translateTwicePositive are separately constructed complex-linear maps from that same domain into the existing positive algebra. Continuity follows by composition; gauge invariance follows by the actual shifted vertex gauge. The second map does not iterate an operator assumed to preserve the margin domain.
- **Change-of-variables direction:** for arbitrary actual continuous F,G the substitution U=S V and proved S Theta S=Theta give B(tau F,G)=B(F,tau G). The restricted diagonal specialization uses G=tau F and gives B(tau F,tau F)=B(F,tau^2 F), where the required tau^2 F membership is supplied by the two-step endpoint proof. The one-step-margin region 1,...,T-1 does not by itself keep tau^2 F positive; that larger-domain implication is not asserted.
- **Genuine radical consumer:** if F in twoStepDomain has its original positive observable in N, the actual left-annihilation theorem applies to the actual positive member tau^2 F. The diagonal identity gives Re B(tau F,tau F)=0, and the proved null membership equivalence yields tau F in N. No whole-positive-algebra radical invariance is assumed.
- **Exact quotient domain and map:** classMap is the margin-submodule inclusion followed by the existing quotient map q. restrictedClasses is precisely its range in Q=A/N; membership is equivalent to existence of a margin representative F with q(F)=the given class. translatedClass is q composed with the proved first translation. Null preservation proves ker(classMap)<=ker(translatedClass), and in particular equal original classes from this domain have equal translated classes. The maintained liftQ and first isomorphism theorem then construct restrictedTranslation:restrictedClasses->Q. Its representative formula is restrictedTranslation(q(F))=q(tau F). This is a map from the specified image subspace into the full quotient, not an asserted endomorphism of that image or of Q; no representative outside the margin domain is used to define it.
- **Symmetric consumer on the actual common domain:** for q,r in restrictedClasses, quotientReflectionForm(restrictedTranslation(q),r)=quotientReflectionForm(q,restrictedTranslation(r)). The proof returns to margin representatives and the actual integral identity. Translated vectors are not claimed to stay in restrictedClasses. Finite volume does not make this continuous-observable domain finite dimensional, and no density is asserted.
- **Imported upstream mathematics:** the unchanged finite Wilson/reflection/positive-form chain and Mathlib periodic representative arithmetic, full finite sum reindexing, product measure permutation, Bochner change of variables, submodule quotient lift and first isomorphism theorem. The latter retain Johannes Hölzl, Mario Carneiro, Kevin Buzzard and Yury Kudryashov's authorship and Apache-2.0 license; imported product/Haar source retains Floris van Doorn and Sébastien Gouëzel's attribution. Other imported attributions and licenses remain intact.

## Colliding blocks: chord geometry and the velocity terminal sector

- **Source/byline/version:** Grant Sanderson, *Why do colliding blocks compute pi?* (2019), https://www.3blue1brown.com/lessons/clacks-solution/ (video jsYwFizhncE); *Why colliding blocks compute pi* (March 13, 2025), text adaptation by Josh Pullen, https://www.3blue1brown.com/lessons/colliding-blocks-v2/ (video 6dTyOl1fmDo), phase diagram and Counting Collisions sections. G. Galperin, *Playing Pool with π* (2003), DOI https://doi.org/10.1070/RD2003v008n04ABEH000252. Local attribution: grwtsk contributors.
- **Exact source:** `GRWTSK/MechanicsOfParticlesAndSystems/DynamicsOfASystemOfParticlesIncludingCelestialMechanics/CollisionOfRigidOrPseudoRigidBodies/CollisionVelocitySector.lean`, blob `dcaa3d06afed8925f79fc899b776c38cf9b9a8d0`; its containing publication commit binds the complete tree. MSC2020 70F35; namespace `GRWTSK.Mechanics.Classical.ElasticCollision.VelocityPhase` retained.
- **Fifteen source theorem candidates:** `cone_iff_outgoing` identifies the signed weighted-velocity cone with 0<=u<=v for m,M>0; `terminal_of_cone` supplies absence of future impacts. `rotation_chord`, `rotation_chord_double` and `physical_pair_chord` prove exact squared-chord identities, the last consuming two actual isolated Steps and giving 4*(twice kinetic energy)*sin(theta)^2. This is the two-step chord and must not be mislabeled as the distance between every pair of adjacent physical states.
- **Exact branch and physical consumer:** `even_gap`, `even_cone_iff`, `physical_even_outgoing_iff`, `incoming_pairs_even_phase`, `incoming_pairs_terminal_sector`. For R>0, 0<=alpha<pi and 0<theta<pi/2, the even semicircle point (-R*cos(alpha),R*sin(alpha)) is in the terminal cone iff pi<=alpha+theta. Equality is included. Actual paired Steps from velocities (0,-c), c>0, give alpha=2*k*theta and R=sqrt(M)*c. The consumer proves terminality from 2*k*theta<pi<=(2*k+1)*theta, with those branch inequalities explicit. No desired impact-count equality, trace existence, or physical/angular equivalence is a supplied certificate.
- **Odd branch declarations:** `odd_gap`, `reflected_even_coordinates`, `odd_cone_iff`, `incoming_odd_phase`, `incoming_odd_terminal_sector`. One actual block Step after k actual pairs gives beta=(2k+2)*theta and coordinates (-R*cos(beta),-R*sin(beta)). With the previous angle strictly in (0,pi) and beta<=2pi, the terminal cone is entered iff pi<=beta, including equality. The physical consumer assumes (2k+1)*theta<pi<=(2k+2)*theta, not an already-known impact count.
- **Roles and evidence:** existing collision/paired-trace source and upstream real trigonometry are imported mathematics; fifteen local proof bodies are SOURCE_PROOF_CANDIDATES. The sine sign lemma from the unfolding module is a real trigonometric fact, not use of the general physical count theorem. No numerical evidence, new axioms or assumed count fields are added. Kernel-verified bridges: none claimed. Compilation NOT_RUN; fresh kernel/type replay NOT_RUN; transitive axiom audit NOT_RUN by user deferral. Source/whitespace review is separate from acceptance.
- **Precisely remaining route:** the chord identity algebraically replaces part of the inscribed-angle reasoning; the full oriented-arc/inscribed-angle construction is not claimed. Both parity branches now have actual-Step terminal-sector consumers. An all-prefix phase-branch bound and a standalone circle-based existence/count proof remain separate. The existing optics construction supplies physical existence and the general strict-ceiling count. Every-scale decimal-floor equality remains unresolved; fixed-prefix stability cannot be specialized diagonally. No video is marked verified complete.


## Finite SU(2) Wilson: gauge-averaged slice quadratic reduction and semidefiniteness

- **Paper context and public bylines:** Arthur Jaffe and Edward Witten, *Quantum Yang--Mills Theory*, in *The Millennium Prize Problems*, AMS (2006), section 6.5, pp.11--12, stable source https://www.claymath.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/yangmills.pdf. The paper supplies finite-lattice context, not the particular local proof or an accepted unbounded-ratio result. No researcher participation or historical influence is inferred. Local attribution: grwtsk contributors.
- **Exact source and hypotheses:** complete inspected main `fb0007e9c9b73d3e72a7d572e1b85be966f58c2e`, tree `a79759e130770acc3c2356cb921899c8394b5617`; cyclic-marginal publication #1450 is consumed at its actual maintained subject paths. Let L be a nonzero natural period, nu the constructed sliceHaar, dg the constructed sliceGaugeHaar, and V,M,W,K the literal WilsonSliceAction definitions. These are finite products of the original normalized SU2 Haar and the original penalty1-ReTr/2. The quadratic reduction and reality statements allow every real beta. Nonnegativity uses beta>=0. f is an actual continuous complex function on Slice L SU2, with the explicit hypothesis f(sliceGaugeAction(g,a))=f(a) for every actual slice gauge g and a. No L2 or operator hypothesis is supplied. The intended two-slice consumer retains L=2*T,T>=4; the written identities themselves use only nonzero L.
- **Missing step paid by a source proof, not a symmetry inference:** sliceGaugeHomeomorph has the literal forward action g.a and inverse g^(-1).a, proved with sliceGauge_mul. sliceGaugeMeasurableEquiv makes the measurable bijection explicit. measurePreserving_sliceGauge instantiates the existing genuine two-sided product-Haar theorem at each actual spatial link e=(s,i), with left factor g(s) and right factor g(s+e_i)^(-1). Neither a collapse map nor the separate K_gauge theorem stands in for this change of variables.
- **Actual weighted observable and integral:** weightedObservable(beta,f)(a)=M(a)*f(a), with continuity from the maintained M source, and weightedObservable_gauge from the actual M_gauge and f hypothesis. sliceQuadratic is defined to be integral_a integral_b conj(f(a))*K(a,b)*f(b) dnu(b)dnu(a). This is an auxiliary continuous-function quadratic integral, not the existing reflection form q, a norm, or a transfer operator.
- **All Haar/Fubini premises retained:** gaugeIntegrand(a,b,g)=conj(M(a)*f(a))*W(a,g.b)*(M(b)*f(b)). Its full joint continuity and integrability on ((a,b),g), and joint (b,g) integrability at every fixed a, are proved on the actual compact probability spaces. quadraticIntegrand_eq_gaugeIntegral unfolds the exact K and pays complex/real integral coercions and scalar placement. fixedGauge_quadraticIntegral changes b to g.b using its actual measure-preserving measurable equivalence and weightedObservable_gauge. gauge_averaged_quadratic first swaps the b,g integrations using the supplied joint integrability, then applies this fixed-g equality, and only then returns the still-present gauge integral by its proved mass1.
- **Exact reduction:** integral_a integral_b conj(f(a))*K(a,b)*f(b) dnu(b)dnu(a) = integral_a integral_b conj(M(a)*f(a))*W(a,b)*(M(b)*f(b)) dnu(b)dnu(a). There is no extra normalizer, missing temporal gauge layer, positivity certificate or inferred source/grid map. The proof does not use separate gauge invariance of K to infer the integral equality.
- **Literal full-link Gram source:** SliceCoordinate L=SpatialLink L x RealCoordinate. sliceFeatures(a)(e,k)=realCoordinates(a(e),k), retaining all eight real/imaginary matrix-entry coordinates at EVERY actual spatial link. spatialLinkCount=E_s=card(SpatialLink L)=3*L^3 by the maintained cardinality theorem. W_eq_exp_gram proves W(a,b)=exp(-beta*E_s)*exp((beta/2)*Gram(Phi,a,b)). This is a scalar entrywise exponential, not a matrix exponential. sliceGram_abs_le proves |Gram|<=2*E_s by summing the actual SU2 real-trace bound. Coordinate and joint Gram continuity are supplied directly on these actual slice coordinates.
- **Series and integral consumer:** normalizedTaylor_abs_le_one proves the correctly normalized absolute bound for every truncation at beta>=0. W_quadratic_nonnegative consumes the existing FiniteRealGramIntegral.expGram_integral_nonnegative with the actual nu, these full-link features, their continuity, the proved 2*E_s bound and the actual continuous complex M*f. The imported supplier retains every coordinate word and the empty word, nonnegative scalar Taylor coefficients and a fixed-finite-lattice integrable majorant before dominated convergence. W_quadratic_eq_generic pays the swap and W symmetry needed for its opposite conjugation convention. No positivity is inferred from pointwise W>=0.
- **Concrete positive quadratic supplier:** GRWTSK.Geometry.GaugeTheory.WilsonSliceQuadraticPositivity.sliceQuadratic_nonnegative consumes the actual gauge_averaged_quadratic, not an assumed positive-operator field. sliceKernel_quadratic_positive states imaginary part0 and nonnegative real part for the displayed literal K integral. Reality is independently paid by sliceQuadratic_conj using actual K_symm, full integrability, Fubini and integral conjugation; it needs no gauge-invariance or sign restriction on beta. The result is semidefinite.
- **Beta0 and scope boundaries:** sliceQuadratic_zero proves the actual rank-one formula Q_0(f)=|integral f dnu|^2 for every continuous f, preserving zero-mean null vectors. The earlier beta0 translation isometry and T3/period6 support contraction sources remain unchanged. exp(-12*beta*L^3)<=K is still solely a pointwise scalar denominator bound, never an operator/form lower bound, strict positivity or coercivity. Neither these new sources nor their publication constitutes an accepted general interacting counterexample.
- **Actual mathematical consumer and next lemma:** this supplies the positive continuous-function quadratic ingredient for the auxiliary slice integral map in #1449's ordinary ratio argument. The next smallest operator construction is to define the actual K integral on L2(nu), prove representative independence and boundedness from |K|<=1 on the probability space, and prove agreement with the displayed continuous-function formula. Its gauge-invariant closed subspace, density of invariant continuous functions and continuity-based extension of positivity must be paid explicitly. A continuous-function theorem alone does not establish L2 positivity.

No spectral decomposition, eigenfunction sequence, transfer-operator certificate, reflection quotient norm/completion, dense full quotient domain or whole-space time evolution is assumed here.

## Colliding blocks: arbitrary physical prefixes and the first circle turn

- **Source/byline/version:** Grant Sanderson, *Why do colliding blocks compute pi?* (2019), https://www.3blue1brown.com/lessons/clacks-solution/ (video jsYwFizhncE); *Why colliding blocks compute pi* (March 13, 2025), text adaptation by Josh Pullen, https://www.3blue1brown.com/lessons/colliding-blocks-v2/ (video 6dTyOl1fmDo), velocity phase and Counting Collisions sections. G. Galperin, *Playing Pool with π* (2003), DOI https://doi.org/10.1070/RD2003v008n04ABEH000252. Local attribution: grwtsk contributors.
- **Exact source:** `GRWTSK/MechanicsOfParticlesAndSystems/DynamicsOfASystemOfParticlesIncludingCelestialMechanics/CollisionOfRigidOrPseudoRigidBodies/CollisionTracePhase.lean`, blob `2a490b7cbe18dcc49350a37cc03bd74b5cf1091f`; containing publication commit binds the complete source tree. MSC2020 70F35; fully qualified prefix `GRWTSK.Mechanics.Classical.ElasticCollision.VelocityPhase`.
- **Ten source theorem candidates:** `first_event_blocks` uses near-body u>=0 and an actual Step. `even_trace_pairs` and `odd_trace_pairs` use m+M!=0, u>=0 and an actual Trace; they deduce the actual block/wall pair representation, or pairs followed by one actual block Step, from the physical incoming tests and relative-velocity law. These are proved bridges, not assumed event lists. `even_trace_phase` and `odd_trace_phase` assume positive masses and any actual prefix from positions (a,b), velocities (0,-c), and give the exact weighted-circle phase. No positivity/separation of positions or c is needed for those conditional identities.
- **Circle upper bound and first-turn control:** `trace_split` preserves impact count; `trace_from_terminal_zero` excludes nonempty continuations. `ceiling_trace_terminal`, `trace_length_le_angular_ceiling`, `trace_first_turn_bound` use m,M,c>0 and arbitrary initial a,b with an actual Trace. The arithmetic N=(ceil(pi/theta)-1).toNat, theta=atan(sqrt(m/M)), supplies a terminal sector in either parity. Every actual trace of length N is terminal by the circle formula. A trace of length greater than N would split into that terminal prefix and a forbidden nonempty continuation. Therefore every actual prefix satisfies n<=N and n*theta<pi. The general optics trajectory/count theorem is not invoked to supply that bound, a matching trace or a certificate.
- **Roles and evidence:** upstream real arithmetic/trigonometry and the inherited collision/sector source are imported mathematics; ten new local theorem bodies are SOURCE_PROOF_CANDIDATES. No new axioms, numerical evidence or assumed physical/angular bridge is added. Kernel-verified bridges: none claimed. ## Colliding blocks: conservation-law selection and a scoped claim map

- **Sources/bylines/version:** Grant Sanderson, *The most unexpected answer to a counting puzzle* (January 13, 2019), https://www.3blue1brown.com/lessons/clacks/; *Why do colliding blocks compute pi?* (January 20, 2019), https://www.3blue1brown.com/lessons/clacks-solution/; *How colliding blocks act like a beam of light...to compute pi* (February 3, 2019), https://www.3blue1brown.com/lessons/clacks-via-light/; *Why colliding blocks compute pi* (March 13, 2025), https://www.3blue1brown.com/lessons/colliding-blocks-v2/. Published text adaptations where credited are by Josh Pullen; the 2019 solution page supplies no adaptation byline. G. Galperin, *Playing Pool with π* (2003), DOI https://doi.org/10.1070/RD2003v008n04ABEH000252. Local attribution: grwtsk contributors.
- **Exact source:** `GRWTSK/MechanicsOfParticlesAndSystems/DynamicsOfASystemOfParticlesIncludingCelestialMechanics/CollisionOfRigidOrPseudoRigidBodies/CollisionConservationSelection.lean`, blob `dced0871451876e266ea3b84d82adc4ee75d3aaa`; containing publication commit binds this source and the full base `f4a45b5e2d5475d3c32997dd4cc2ca50cce7cf2d`. The passive mathematical claim map `documentation/mathematics/_verification/targets/three-blue-one-brown-collision-claims.json` pins all thirteen collision module blobs and distinguishes individual claims/evidence roles. It is not a verification dispatch, runtime state or receipt. Source placement is MSC2020 70F35; namespaces are preserved.
- **Nine declaration candidates:** under `GRWTSK.Mechanics.Classical.ElasticCollision`, `energy_momentum_identity`, `relative_speed_sq_of_conservation`, `conservation_dichotomy`, `conservation_iff_two_branches`, `collision_of_conservation_nontrivial`, `collision_of_conservation_separating`, `incoming_collision_changes_velocities`. Positive masses and conserved momentum/energy yield the unchanged branch OR the explicit collision law. Nontrivial change or incoming v<u plus separating U<=V selects the collision; relative-velocity reversal is a conclusion of the selected law rather than a supplied premise. The branches may coincide at u=v, and conservation alone does not exclude that case. `GRWTSK.Mechanics.Classical.CollisionAngleCount.equal_mass_angle` proves angle pi/4 for m>0. `GRWTSK.Mechanics.Classical.ElasticCollision.VelocityPhase.equal_mass_general_three_impact_run` constructs a terminal three-impact Trace and unique terminal count for arbitrary equal m>0,0<a<b,c>0, retaining the strict grazing endpoint.
- **Claim-map scope:** the four published lesson texts' central count, physical circle/optics routes, finite-width normalization, explicit examples, approximation and fixed-prefix claims now have named candidate suppliers. Literal oriented-arc/inscribed-angle geometry is separated from the algebraic chord replacement. The all-exponents decimal-floor premise remains unresolved, not a local axiom. Published text mapping does not establish full spoken-video/transcript coverage; the 2019 solution page exposes little mathematical text. Illustrative sound, astrophysical comparisons and implementation correctness are not silently recorded as mathematical theorems. No video is marked complete, and these four lessons do not cover the remaining channel/guest programme.
- **Roles and evidence:** inherited local source and upstream real algebra/trigonometry are imported mathematics; nine new local theorem bodies are SOURCE_PROOF_CANDIDATES. Kernel-verified bridges: none claimed. No numerical simulation, new axiom or conditional target-count interface is added. Compilation NOT_RUN; fresh kernel/type replay NOT_RUN; transitive axiom audit NOT_RUN by user deferral. The static claim-map/diff review is not acceptance. Full source-claim audit, literal missing method and eventual exact-source checking remain separate; no native/replay dispatch or root/dependency changes.
